/Users/virtualmachine/.codex/sessions/2026/02/12/rollout-2026-02-12T22-28-59-019c55b0-3cc9-7491-81cb-dc9b614f2b48.jsonl:83:{"timestamp":"2026-02-13T06:30:11.144Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"function_call_output","call_id":"call_QhnpnkYClNBZrVmkOKjkw9hD","output":"Chunk ID: f935e3\nWall time: 0.0529 seconds\nProcess exited with code 0\nOriginal token count: 2524\nOutput:\n# AGTMUX Spec (v0.5)\n\nDate: 2026-02-13\nStatus: Draft\n\n## 1. Background\n\nCurrent workflows rely on tmux for organizing work by session/window/pane, while agent CLIs (Claude, Codex, Gemini) run in panes.\nThe current pain is that operator awareness depends on manual polling: checking panes repeatedly to know whether agents are running, waiting for user input, completed, or idle.\n\nThe desired direction is:\n- tcmux-like visibility for tmux + agent state\n- multi-agent support (Claude, Codex, Gemini)\n- adapter-based extensibility for future agents (for example Copilot CLI, Cursor CLI)\n- grouping and summaries by tmux session/window\n- unified view across host and VMs (host, vm1, vm2)\n- eventual macOS resident app for fast control actions\n\n## 2. Problems\n\n- No always-on unified status view across panes.\n- Manual polling is expensive and error-prone.\n- Agent state semantics differ per CLI and are not normalized.\n- tmux structures can get noisy as sessions/windows/panes grow.\n- There is no single control surface for `send`, `attach`, `view output`, and `kill`.\n- Host/VM contexts are separated operationally, reducing global awareness.\n\n## 3. Goals\n\n- Provide always-available state visibility for agent panes without manual polling.\n- Support Claude, Codex, and Gemini with a unified state model.\n- Provide tcmux-like listing commands:\n  - list agent panes\n  - list windows (including agent status summary)\n  - list agent sessions\n- Unify host and VM targets in one view while preserving target identity.\n- Categorize by status (running, waiting_input, waiting_approval, completed, idle, error, unknown).\n- Categorize cross-target by tmux session for quick project-level awareness.\n- Default grouping must be `target-session` to avoid cross-target session-name collisions.\n- Enable operational actions from one tooling surface:\n  - send\n  - attach\n  - view output\n  - kill\n- Keep architecture reusable so the same backend powers both CLI and future macOS resident app.\n\n## 4. Non-Goals (Initial)\n\n- Replacing tmux itself.\n- Building a full terminal emulator UI.\n- Perfect semantic understanding of every possible third-party CLI output format.\n- Cloud sync or multi-host distributed orchestration in v0.\n\n## 5. User Stories\n\n1. As a developer, I want to see all agent panes and their states at once across host and VMs, so I can stop checking each pane manually.\n2. As a developer, I want a session-level summary grouped across targets, so I can quickly decide which project area needs attention.\n3. As a developer, I want to filter by state (for example waiting_input/waiting_approval), so I can prioritize intervention.\n4. As a developer, I want to attach to the exact pane where an agent is waiting.\n5. As a developer, I want to send input to a pane from CLI/app without switching context.\n6. As a developer, I want to inspect recent output and then kill or resume the agent safely.\n\n## 6. Requirements\n\n### 6.1 Functional Requirements\n\n- FR-1: Detect and persist agent states for Claude and Codex in MVP, and Gemini by Phase 2.\n- FR-2: Normalize agent-specific signals into a common state model.\n- FR-3: Maintain mapping between tmux pane and agent runtime metadata.\n- FR-4: Provide pane/window/session listing with state summaries.\n- FR-5: Support control commands: send, attach, view-output, kill.\n- FR-6: Offer machine-readable output (`--json`) for all list commands.\n- FR-7: Include session/window grouping and counts by state.\n- FR-8: Include a reconciliation mechanism so stale states self-heal.\n- FR-9: Support multiple targets (host/vm1/vm2) in one aggregated view.\n- FR-10: Provide target commands (`add`, `connect`, `list`, `remove`) and target-scoped operations.\n- FR-11: Provide adapter registry so new agent CLIs can be added without changing core state engine.\n- FR-12: Support future adapters such as Copilot CLI and Cursor CLI.\n- FR-13: Introduce runtime identity guard (`runtime_id`/`pane_epoch`) to prevent stale action/event application.\n- FR-14: Define deterministic event dedupe and ordering semantics.\n- FR-15: All actions must enforce fail-closed preconditions via server-side snapshot validation.\n- FR-16: Define canonical action reference grammar and unambiguous resolution rules.\n\n### 6.2 Non-Functional Requirements\n\n- NFR-1: Near real-time updates (target: <= 2 seconds visible lag).\n- NFR-2: Safe failure mode: unknown state instead of incorrect definitive state.\n- NFR-3: Low overhead on host CPU/memory.\n- NFR-4: Idempotent event handling and robust against duplicate events.\n- NFR-5: Extensible adapter model for future agents.\n- NFR-6: Adapter contract stability (backward-compatible interface across minor versions).\n- NFR-7: Partial-result operation under target failures (do not block global listings).\n- NFR-8: Deterministic convergence for same input event stream.\n- NFR-9: Sensitive connection data must not be stored in plaintext state DB.\n\n## 7. Specification\n\n### 7.1 Core Architecture\n\n- Target Manager:\n  - stores known targets (`host`, `vm1`, `vm2`, ...).\n  - manages connectivity and remote command execution context.\n- Target Executor:\n  - unified local/ssh execution abstraction for tmux operations and adapter runtime.\n  - required for all read/write operations to targets.\n- Collector (per target):\n  - collects tmux topology and adapter signals from one target.\n- Aggregator / Daemon (`agtmuxd`):\n  - merges events from collectors.\n  - owns state engine and persistence.\n  - serves read/write API for CLI and future macOS app.\n- Agent Adapter Registry:\n  - maps `agent_type` to adapter implementation and capabilities.\n  - core engine only depends on adapter interface, not concrete agent logic.\n- Agent Adapters (initial):\n  - Claude adapter: hook-driven events.\n  - Codex adapter: notify-driven events plus wrapper lifecycle signals.\n  - Gemini adapter: wrapper lifecycle + output parser signals.\n  - future adapters: Copilot CLI, Cursor CLI (no core redesign required).\n- Tmux Observer:\n  - tracks pane/session/window topology per target via tmux metadata.\n- State Engine:\n  - merges adapter events and tmux observations into canonical state.\n- State Store:\n  - durable store (SQLite recommended) as the single source of truth.\n- Presentation Layer:\n  - CLI commands now.\n  - macOS resident app later, reading the same daemon API.\n\n### 7.2 Canonical State Model\n\nCanonical states:\n- `running`\n- `waiting_input`\n- `waiting_approval`\n- `completed`\n- `idle`\n- `error`\n- `unknown`\n\nState precedence (highest first):\n1. `error`\n2. `waiting_approval`\n3. `waiting_input`\n4. `running`\n5. `completed`\n6. `idle`\n7. `unknown`\n\nDefaults (recommended):\n- `completed` auto-demotes to `idle` after 120 seconds (configurable).\n- `kill` default signal is `INT`.\n\nNotes:\n- `completed` means last task ended and is still fresh for operator recognition.\n- Resolution order is `dedupe/order check -> runtime guard -> freshness check -> precedence`.\n- Unknown or stale information must not be promoted to a confident active state.\n\n### 7.2.1 Transition and Safety Rules\n\n- Every event must include enough metadata to dedupe and order safely.\n- Event application must be dropped if runtime identity does not match current pane runtime.\n- Precedence is applied only among fresh candidate signals.\n- Demotion job (`completed -> idle`) must include a version/runtime guard.\n- Demotion time base is daemon `ingested_at` (not remote wall clock).\n- `unknown` must include `reason_code` (for example `stale_signal`, `target_unreachable`, `unsupported_signal`).\n- If target health is `down` or signals are stale beyond TTL, state resolution must short-circuit to `unknown`.\n\n### 7.2.2 Ordering and Dedupe Algorithm\n\n- Event apply order is deterministic:\n  1. reject duplicates by `(runtime_id, source, dedupe_key)`\n  2. compare ordering key:\n     - `source_seq` when available (same `runtime_id + source`)\n     - then `event_time`\n     - then `ingested_at`\n     - then `event_id`\n  3. apply only if key is newer than stored cursor for that `runtime_id + source`\n- A source-specific cursor must be maintained to avoid cross-source starvation.\n\n### 7.2.3 Adapter Contract\n\nEach adapter MUST implement a common contract:\n- `ContractVersion() -> string`\n- `IdentifyProcess(ctx, pane) -> match_result`\n- `Subscribe(ctx, pane) -> signal_stream` (for event-driven adapters)\n- `Poll(ctx, pane) -> []signal` (for polling fallback)\n- `Normalize(signal) -> state_transition`\n- `Health(ctx) -> status`\n\nCapabilities are declared by adapter:\n- `event_driven`\n- `polling_required`\n- `supports_waiting_approval`\n- `supports_waiting_input`\n- `supports_completed`\n\nCore behavior:\n- State Engine consumes only normalized transitions.\n- Unknown or unsupported signals must degrade to `unknown`, never fabricated states.\n\n### 7.3 Data Model (SQLite draft)\n\n- `targets`\n  - `target_id` (PK)\n  - `target_name` (`host`, `vm1`, `vm2`, ...)\n  - `kind` (`local`/`ssh`)\n  - `connection_ref` (non-secret reference such as ssh host alias)\n  - `is_default`\n  - `last_seen_at`\n  - `health` (`ok`/`degraded`/`down`)\n  - `updated_at`\n- `panes`\n  - `target_id`\n  - `pane_id`\n  - `session_name`\n  - `window_id`\n  - `window_name`\n  - `updated_at`\n  - PK: (`target_id`, `pane_id`)\n- `runtimes`\n  - `runtime_id` (PK)\n  - `target_id`\n  - `pane_id`\n  - `pane_epoch`\n  - `agent_type`\n  - `pid` (nullable)\n  - `started_at`\n  - `ended_at` (nullable)\n  - UNIQUE: (`target_id`, `pane_id`, `pane_epoch`)\n- `events`\n  - `event_id` (PK)\n  - `runtime_id`\n  - `event_type`\n  - `source` (`hook`/`notify`/`wrapper`/`poller`)\n  - `source_event_id` (nullable)\n  - `source_seq` (nullable)\n  - `event_time`\n  - `ingested_at`\n  - `dedupe_key`\n  - `raw_payload`\n  - UNIQUE: (`runtime_id`, `source`, `dedupe_key`)\n- `runtime_source_cursors`\n  - `runtime_id`\n  - `source`\n  - `last_source_seq` (nullable)\n  - `last_order_event_time`\n  - `last_order_ingested_at`\n  - `last_order_event_id`\n  - PK: (`runtime_id`, `source`)\n- `states`\n  - `target_id`\n  - `pane_id`\n  - `runtime_id`\n  - `state`\n  - `reason_code`\n  - `confidence` (`high`/`medium`/`low`)\n  - `state_version`\n  - `last_source_seq` (nullable)\n  - `last_seen_at`\n"}}

However, in Default mode you should strongly prefer executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions.\n"}},"effort":"xhigh","summary":"auto","user_instructions":"# AGENTS.md\n\nこのリポジトリは AGTMUX v2 の再始動用です。実装・設計の判断は `docs/v2` を正本にします。\n\n## 1. 最初に読む順番（必須）\n\n1. `docs/v2/00-index.md`\n2. `docs/v2/10-product-charter.md`\n3. `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n4. `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n5. `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n\n`docs/v2/references/*` は必要時のみ参照。\n\n## 2. 絶対に守る不変条件\n\n1. `selected pane = stream-only`\n2. active path で snapshot と stream を混在させない\n3. local echo をしない\n4. cursor/IME/scroll を app 側で推定しない\n5. data plane と control plane を分離する\n6. `wezterm-gui fork` 一本（thin integration は採用しない）\n7. fork改造範囲は `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md` に従う\n\n## 3. 変更管理ルール\n\n変更は次の3分類で扱う。\n\n1. `Patch`: 挙動不変の修正\n2. `Policy`: UX/運用ルール変更\n3. `Constitutional`: 不変条件に触る変更\n\n`Policy` 以上の変更では、実装前に docs を更新し、影響範囲と受け入れ条件を明記する。\n\n## 4. ドキュメント正本\n\n1. 仕様方針: `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n2. Data Model / protocol: `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n3. 実装順序 / gate: `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n4. 教訓 / 再発防止: `docs/v2/50-poc-learnings.md`\n5. wire/gate/bootstrap: `docs/v2/specs/*`\n6. 意思決定: `docs/v2/adr/*`\n7. fork境界: `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md`\n\n過去資料は `docs/v2/references/*` に隔離し、通常作業では読まない。\n\n## 5. 実装時の最小コンテキスト方針\n\n1. まず必読5ファイルのみ読む\n2. 追加資料を読む場合は「読む理由」を1行で残す\n3. 無関係ファイルの探索を避ける\n\n## 6. PR/変更前チェック\n\n1. 不変条件を破っていない\n2. 変更内容に応じた docs 更新を行った\n3. 受け入れ条件（AC）を定義した\n4. rollback 手順を定義した\n5. `docs/v2/00-index.md` の導線を壊していない\n\n## 7. ファイル配置ルール\n\n1. 新規設計文書は `docs/v2` 配下に追加する\n2. 番号付き命名を維持する（`60-` 以降を使用）\n3. 実験メモや比較資料は `docs/v2/references` に置く\n\n\n## Skills\nA skill is a set of local instructions to follow that is stored in a `SKILL.md` file.    418	{"timestamp":"2026-02-22T03:33:46.911Z","type":"turn_context","payload":{"turn_id":"019c8367-618d-76c0-9049-cb8e00208a06","cwd":"/Users/virtualmachine/ghq/github.com/g960059/agtmux/.worktrees/exp/v2-rust-bootstrap","approval_policy":"never","sandbox_policy":{"type":"danger-full-access"},"model":"gpt-5.3-codex","personality":"pragmatic","collaboration_mode":{"mode":"default","settings":{"model":"gpt-5.3-codex","reasoning_effort":"xhigh","developer_instructions":"# Collaboration Mode: Default\n\nYou are now in Default mode. Known mode names are Default and Plan.\n\n## request_user_input availability\n\nThe `request_user_input` tool is unavailable in Default mode. However, in Default mode you should strongly prefer executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions.\n"}},"effort":"xhigh","summary":"auto","user_instructions":"# AGENTS.md\n\nこのリポジトリは AGTMUX v2 の再始動用です。実装・設計の判断は `docs/v2` を正本にします。\n\n## 1. 最初に読む順番（必須）\n\n1. `docs/v2/00-index.md`\n2. `docs/v2/10-product-charter.md`\n3. `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n4. `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n5. `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n\n`docs/v2/references/*` は必要時のみ参照。\n\n## 2. 絶対に守る不変条件\n\n1. `selected pane = stream-only`\n2. active path で snapshot と stream を混在させない\n3. local echo をしない\n4. cursor/IME/scroll を app 側で推定しない\n5. data plane と control plane を分離する\n6. `wezterm-gui fork` 一本（thin integration は採用しない）\n7. fork改造範囲は `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md` に従う\n\n## 3. 変更管理ルール\n\n変更は次の3分類で扱う。\n\n1. `Patch`: 挙動不変の修正\n2. `Policy`: UX/運用ルール変更\n3. `Constitutional`: 不変条件に触る変更\n\n`Policy` 以上の変更では、実装前に docs を更新し、影響範囲と受け入れ条件を明記する。\n\n## 4. ドキュメント正本\n\n1. 仕様方針: `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n2. Data Model / protocol: `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n3. 実装順序 / gate: `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n4. 教訓 / 再発防止: `docs/v2/50-poc-learnings.md`\n5. wire/gate/bootstrap: `docs/v2/specs/*`\n6. 意思決定: `docs/v2/adr/*`\n7. fork境界: `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md`\n\n過去資料は `docs/v2/references/*` に隔離し、通常作業では読まない。\n\n## 5. 実装時の最小コンテキスト方針\n\n1. まず必読5ファイルのみ読む\n2. 追加資料を読む場合は「読む理由」を1行で残す\n3. 無関係ファイルの探索を避ける\n\n## 6. PR/変更前チェック\n\n1. 不変条件を破っていない\n2. 変更内容に応じた docs 更新を行った\n3. 受け入れ条件（AC）を定義した\n4. rollback 手順を定義した\n5. `docs/v2/00-index.md` の導線を壊していない\n\n## 7. ファイル配置ルール\n\n1. 新規設計文書は `docs/v2` 配下に追加する\n2. 番号付き命名を維持する（`60-` 以降を使用）\n3. 実験メモや比較資料は `docs/v2/references` に置く\n\n\n## Skills\nA skill is a set of local instructions to follow that is stored in a `SKILL.md` file. Below is the list of skills that can be used. Each entry includes a name, description, and file path so you can open the source for full instructions when using a specific skill.\n### Available skills\n- ci-governance: Standardize quality gates for TypeScript repositories using just, Husky, PR verify, optional preview deploy, and gated main deploy. Use this when setting up or fixing CI/CD quality policy. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/ci-governance/SKILL.md)\n- codex: Codex CLI（OpenAI）を使用してコードや文言について相談・レビューを行う。 トリガー: \"codex\", \"codexと相談\", \"codexに聞いて\", \"コードレビュー\", \"レビューして\" 使用場面: (1) 文言・メッセージの検討、(2) コードレビュー、(3) 設計の相談、(4) バグ調査、(5) 解消困難な問題の調査 (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex/SKILL.md)\n- codex-implementation-review: Review actual code changes with Codex after implementation. Use for bug risk, regression risk, missing tests, and merge readiness checks. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-implementation-review/SKILL.md)\n- codex-plan-review: Review implementation plans with Codex before coding. Use when validating architecture, scope, risks, and test strategy for medium-to-large changes. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-plan-review/SKILL.md)\n- deploy-cloudrun: Configure Cloud Run deployments with GitHub Actions using WIF auth, reusable verify workflow, PR preview services, and cleanup on PR close or preview label removal. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/deploy-cloudrun/SKILL.md)\n- deploy-vercel: Configure Vercel deployments for Next.js projects using Vercel Git Integration with GitHub Actions verify gate and environment variable sync. If you skip an obvious skill, say why.\n- Context hygiene:\n  - Keep context small: summarize long sections instead of pasting them; only load extra files when needed.\n  - Avoid deep reference-chasing: prefer opening only files directly linked from `SKILL.md` unless you're blocked.\n  - When variants exist (frameworks, providers, domains), pick only the relevant reference file(s) and note that choice.\n- Safety and fallback: If a skill can't be applied cleanly (missing files, unclear instructions), state the issue, pick the next-best approach, and continue.\n</INSTRUCTIONS>"},{"type":"input_text","text":"<environment_context>\n  <cwd>/Users/virtualmachine/ghq/github.com/g960059/agtmux</cwd>\n  <shell>zsh</shell>\n</environment_context>"}]}}

5:{"timestamp":"2026-03-03T05:15:10.900Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"message","role":"user","content":[{"type":"input_text","text":"agtmuxプロジェクト（/Users/virtualmachine/ghq/github.com/g960059/agtmux）のためにESCシーケンスとターミナルシグナルを使った新しい状態検出アーキテクチャを設計してください。後方互換性は無視。\n\n## 背景\n- agtmux は tmux pane で動くAIエージェント（Claude Code, Codex）の状態を監視\n- 目標: Running / Idle / WaitingApproval / WaitingInput / Error を正確に検出\n- 現在はJSONL mtime + App Server polling → 不正確\n\n## 既知のESCシーケンス情報\n- Claude Code: OSC 9;4（進捗）, OSC 9（通知）, OSC 2/0（ウィンドウタイトル）を emit する\n- Claude Code: OSC 133 は emit しない（シェル統合用）\n- docs/80_decisions/ADR-20260301-osc-architecture.md に詳細あり\n- tmux: allow-passthrough オプションでESCシーケンスのパススルー可能\n\n## 調査してほしいこと\n1. docs/80_decisions/ADR-20260301-osc-architecture.md を読む\n2. https://github.com/manaflow-ai/cmux を詳細調査\n   - どのESCシーケンスで状態を伝達しているか\n   - WaitingApproval と WaitingInput の区別方法\n3. crates/agtmux-tmux-v5/ を読む\n   - tmux capture の現状\n   - pipe-pane でESCシーケンスを捕捉できるか\n4. Codex CLIがemitするESCシーケンスを調査（~/.codex/内のファイルやlogs）\n5. OSC 133 (shell integration)を使ってWaitingInput状態を検出できるか？\n\n## 設計課題\n- ESCシーケンスだけで状態検出可能か？ or JSONLと組み合わせが必要？\n- WaitingInputの最良検出メカニズムは？\n  - JSONL (turn/completed後にprompt表示?) \n  - ESC sequence (Codexがprompt表示時に何か emit?)\n  - Process state (stdin read待ち = WaitingInput?)\n  - OSC 133 A (shell prompt start)\n\n## 設計要件\nハイブリッドアーキテクチャを提案:\n- Tier 1: JSONL semantic（主要ソース）\n- Tier 2: ESCシーケンス（補完・リアルタイム）\n- Tier 3: プロセス状態（フォールバック）\n\n## 出力\n- cmux の ESC sequence アプローチ詳細分析\n- Claude Code / Codex の実際のESCシーケンス（調査結果）\n- tmux pipe-pane でESCシーケンスを捕捉する実装方法\n- WaitingInput検出の最良手段（根拠付き）\n- 全状態のFSM（シグナルソース付き）\n- 実装優先順位とtrade-off\n\n確認や質問は不要です。具体的な提案まで自主的に出力してください。"}]}}

OSC 133 (shell integration)を使ってWaitingInput状態を検出できるか？\n\n## 設計課題\n- ESCシーケンスだけで状態検出可能か？ or JSONLと組み合わせが必要？\n- WaitingInputの最良検出メカニズムは？\n  - JSONL (turn/completed後にprompt表示?) \n  - ESC sequence (Codexがprompt表示時に何か emit?)\n  - Process state (stdin read待ち = WaitingInput?)\n  - OSC 133 A (shell prompt start)\n\n## 設計要件\nハイブリッドアーキテクチャを提案:\n- Tier 1: JSONL semantic（主要ソース）\n- Tier 2: ESCシーケンス（補完・リアルタイム）\n- Tier 3: プロセス状態（フォールバック）\n\n## 出力\n- cmux の ESC sequence アプローチ詳細分析\n- Claude Code / Codex の実際のESCシーケンス（調査結果）\n- tmux pipe-pane でESCシーケンスを捕捉する実装方法\n- WaitingInput検出の最良手段（根拠付き）\n- 全状態のFSM（シグナルソース付き）\n- 実装優先順位とtrade-off\n\n確認や質問は不要です。具体的な提案まで自主的に出力してください。","images":[],"local_images":[],"text_elements":[]}}

3:{"timestamp":"2026-02-22T03:26:02.865Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"message","role":"user","content":[{"type":"input_text","text":"# AGENTS.md instructions for /Users/virtualmachine/ghq/github.com/g960059/agtmux/.worktrees/exp/v2-rust-bootstrap\n\n<INSTRUCTIONS>\n# AGENTS.md\n\nこのリポジトリは AGTMUX v2 の再始動用です。実装・設計の判断は `docs/v2` を正本にします。\n\n## 1. 最初に読む順番（必須）\n\n1. `docs/v2/00-index.md`\n2. `docs/v2/10-product-charter.md`\n3. `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n4. `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n5. `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n\n`docs/v2/references/*` は必要時のみ参照。\n\n## 2. 絶対に守る不変条件\n\n1. `selected pane = stream-only`\n2. active path で snapshot と stream を混在させない\n3. local echo をしない\n4. cursor/IME/scroll を app 側で推定しない\n5. data plane と control plane を分離する\n6. `wezterm-gui fork` 一本（thin integration は採用しない）\n7. fork改造範囲は `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md` に従う\n\n## 3. 変更管理ルール\n\n変更は次の3分類で扱う。\n\n1. `Patch`: 挙動不変の修正\n2. `Policy`: UX/運用ルール変更\n3. `Constitutional`: 不変条件に触る変更\n\n`Policy` 以上の変更では、実装前に docs を更新し、影響範囲と受け入れ条件を明記する。\n\n## 4. ドキュメント正本\n\n1. 仕様方針: `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n2. Data Model / protocol: `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n3. 実装順序 / gate: `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n4. 教訓 / 再発防止: `docs/v2/50-poc-learnings.md`\n5. wire/gate/bootstrap: `docs/v2/specs/*`\n6. 意思決定: `docs/v2/adr/*`\n7. fork境界: `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md`\n\n過去資料は `docs/v2/references/*` に隔離し、通常作業では読まない。\n\n## 5. 実装時の最小コンテキスト方針\n\n1. まず必読5ファイルのみ読む\n2. 追加資料を読む場合は「読む理由」を1行で残す\n3. 無関係ファイルの探索を避ける\n\n## 6. PR/変更前チェック\n\n1. 不変条件を破っていない\n2. 変更内容に応じた docs 更新を行った\n3. 受け入れ条件（AC）を定義した\n4. rollback 手順を定義した\n5. `docs/v2/00-index.md` の導線を壊していない\n\n## 7. ファイル配置ルール\n\n1. 新規設計文書は `docs/v2` 配下に追加する\n2. 番号付き命名を維持する（`60-` 以降を使用）\n3. 実験メモや比較資料は `docs/v2/references` に置く\n\n\n## Skills\nA skill is a set of local instructions to follow that is stored in a `SKILL.md` file. Below is the list of skills that can be used. Each entry includes a name, description, and file path so you can open the source for full instructions when using a specific skill.\n### Available skills\n- ci-governance: Standardize quality gates for TypeScript repositories using just, Husky, PR verify, optional preview deploy, and gated main deploy. Use this when setting up or fixing CI/CD quality policy. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/ci-governance/SKILL.md)\n- codex: Codex CLI（OpenAI）を使用してコードや文言について相談・レビューを行う。 トリガー: \"codex\", \"codexと相談\", \"codexに聞いて\", \"コードレビュー\", \"レビューして\" 使用場面: (1) 文言・メッセージの検討、(2) コードレビュー、(3) 設計の相談、(4) バグ調査、(5) 解消困難な問題の調査 (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex/SKILL.md)\n- codex-implementation-review: Review actual code changes with Codex after implementation. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-implementation-review/SKILL.md)\n- codex-plan-review: Review implementation plans with Codex before coding. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-plan-review/SKILL.md)\n- deploy-cloudrun: Configure Cloud Run deployments with GitHub Actions using WIF auth, reusable verify workflow, PR preview services, and cleanup on PR close or preview label removal. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/deploy-cloudrun/SKILL.md)\n- deploy-vercel: Configure Vercel deployments for Next.js projects using Vercel Git Integration with GitHub Actions verify gate and environment variable sync. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.codex/skills/.system/skill-installer/SKILL.md)\n### How to use skills\n- Discovery: The list above is the skills available in this session (name + description + file path). Skill bodies live on disk at the listed paths.\n- Trigger rules: If the user names a skill (with `$SkillName` or plain text) OR the task clearly matches a skill's description shown above, you must use that skill for that turn. Do not carry skills across turns unless re-mentioned.\n- Missing/blocked: If a named skill isn't in the list or the path can't be read, say so briefly and continue with the best fallback.\n- How to use a skill (progressive disclosure):\n  1) After deciding to use a skill, open its `SKILL.md`. Read only enough to follow the workflow.\n  2) When `SKILL.md` references relative paths (e.g., `scripts/foo.py`), resolve them relative to the skill directory listed above first, and only consider other paths if needed.\n  3) If `SKILL.md` points to extra folders such as `references/`, load only the specific files needed for the request; don't bulk-load everything.\n  4) If `scripts/` exist, prefer running or patching them instead of retyping large code blocks.\n  5) If `assets/` or templates exist, reuse them instead of recreating from scratch.\n- Coordination and sequencing:\n  - If multiple skills apply, choose the minimal set that covers the request and state the order you'll use them.\n  - Announce which skill(s) you're using and why (one short line). If you skip an obvious skill, say why.\n- Context hygiene:\n  - Keep context small: summarize long sections instead of pasting them; only load extra files when needed.\n  - Avoid deep reference-chasing: prefer opening only files directly linked from `SKILL.md` unless you're blocked.\n  - When variants exist (frameworks, providers, domains), pick only the relevant reference file(s) and note that choice.\n- Safety and fallback: If a skill can't be applied cleanly (missing files, unclear instructions), state the issue, pick the next-best approach, and continue.\n</INSTRUCTIONS>"}]}}

Any previous instructions for other modes (e.g. Plan mode) are no longer active.\n\nYour active mode changes only when new developer instructions with a different `<collaboration_mode>...</collaboration_mode>` change it; user requests or tool descriptions do not change mode by themselves. Known mode names are Default and Plan.\n\n## request_user_input availability\n\nThe `request_user_input` tool is unavailable in Default mode. If you call it while in Default mode, it will return an error.\n\nIf a decision is necessary and cannot be discovered from local context, ask the user directly. However, in Default mode you should strongly prefer executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions.\n"}},"effort":"xhigh","summary":"auto","user_instructions":"# AGENTS.md\n\nこのリポジトリは AGTMUX v2 の再始動用です。実装・設計の判断は `docs/v2` を正本にします。\n\n## 1. 最初に読む順番（必須）\n\n1. `docs/v2/00-index.md`\n2. `docs/v2/10-product-charter.md`\n3. `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n4. `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n5. `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n\n`docs/v2/references/*` は必要時のみ参照。\n\n## 2. 絶対に守る不変条件\n\n1. `selected pane = stream-only`\n2. active path で snapshot と stream を混在させない\n3. local echo をしない\n4. cursor/IME/scroll を app 側で推定しない\n5. data plane と control plane を分離する\n6. `wezterm-gui fork` 一本（thin integration は採用しない）\n7. fork改造範囲は `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md` に従う\n\n## 3. 変更管理ルール\n\n変更は次の3分類で扱う。\n\n1. `Patch`: 挙動不変の修正\n2. `Policy`: UX/運用ルール変更\n3. `Constitutional`: 不変条件に触る変更\n\n`Policy` 以上の変更では、実装前に docs を更新し、影響範囲と受け入れ条件を明記する。\n\n## 4. ドキュメント正本\n\n1. 仕様方針: `docs/v2/20-unified-design.md`\n2. Data Model / protocol: `docs/v2/30-detailed-design.md`\n3. 実装順序 / gate: `docs/v2/40-execution-plan.md`\n4. 教訓 / 再発防止: `docs/v2/50-poc-learnings.md`\n5. wire/gate/bootstrap: `docs/v2/specs/*`\n6. 意思決定: `docs/v2/adr/*`\n7. fork境界: `docs/v2/specs/74-fork-surface-map.md`\n\n過去資料は `docs/v2/references/*` に隔離し、通常作業では読まない。\n\n## 5. 実装時の最小コンテキスト方針\n\n1. まず必読5ファイルのみ読む\n2. 追加資料を読む場合は「読む理由」を1行で残す\n3. 無関係ファイルの探索を避ける\n\n## 6. PR/変更前チェック\n\n1. 不変条件を破っていない\n2. 変更内容に応じた docs 更新を行った\n3. 受け入れ条件（AC）を定義した\n4. rollback 手順を定義した\n5. `docs/v2/00-index.md` の導線を壊していない\n\n## 7. ファイル配置ルール\n\n1. 新規設計文書は `docs/v2` 配下に追加する\n2. 番号付き命名を維持する（`60-` 以降を使用）\n3. 実験メモや比較資料は `docs/v2/references` に置く\n\n\n## Skills\nA skill is a set of local instructions to follow that is stored in a `SKILL.md` file. Below is the list of skills that can be used. Each entry includes a name, description, and file path so you can open the source for full instructions when using a specific skill.\n### Available skills\n- ci-governance: Standardize quality gates for TypeScript repositories using just, Husky, PR verify, optional preview deploy, and gated main deploy. Use this when setting up or fixing CI/CD quality policy. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/ci-governance/SKILL.md)\n- codex: Codex CLI（OpenAI）を使用してコードや文言について相談・レビューを行う。 トリガー: \"codex\", \"codexと相談\", \"codexに聞いて\", \"コードレビュー\", \"レビューして\" 使用場面: (1) 文言・メッセージの検討、(2) コードレビュー、(3) 設計の相談、(4) バグ調査、(5) 解消困難な問題の調査 (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex/SKILL.md)\n- codex-implementation-review: Review actual code changes with Codex after implementation. Use for bug risk, regression risk, missing tests, and merge readiness checks. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-implementation-review/SKILL.md)\n- codex-plan-review: Review implementation plans with Codex before coding. Use when validating architecture, scope, risks, and test strategy for medium-to-large changes. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/codex-plan-review/SKILL.md)\n- deploy-cloudrun: Configure Cloud Run deployments with GitHub Actions using WIF auth, reusable verify workflow, PR preview services, and cleanup on PR close or preview label removal. (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/deploy-vercel/SKILL.md)\n- gemini: Gemini CLI（Google）を使用してコードや文言について相談・レビューを行う。 トリガー: \"gemini\", \"geminiと相談\", \"geminiに聞いて\" 使用場面: (1) 文言・メッセージの検討、(2) コードレビュー、(3) 設計の相談、(4) バグ調査、(5) 解消困難な問題の調査 (file: /Users/virtualmachine/.agents/skills/gemini/SKILL.md)\n- issue-worktree: Worktree-based workflow for issue-driven development. 1 issue = 1 worktree. thinking
**Summarizing detailed architecture plan**
codex
**結論**
ESCシーケンス単独では `Running / Idle / WaitingApproval / WaitingInput / Error` の高精度判定はできません。  
最適解は、あなたの指定どおり **Tier1: semantic(JSONL/AppServer) + Tier2: ESC/画面信号 + Tier3: process fallback** です。

**1) cmux の ESC アプローチ分析**
1. cmux は以下を解釈しています。  
`OSC 2`（title）、`OSC 9;4`（progress）、独自 `ESC;...BEL`（`PROCESS_START/END`, `PROVIDER_STARTED/STOPPED`, `STATE_UPDATE`）。  
根拠: [ControlSequence.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Sources/cmux-core/ControlSequence.swift), [ControlSequenceTests.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Tests/cmux-coreTests/ControlSequenceTests.swift)
2. `WaitingApproval / WaitingInput` の主判定は ESC ではなく **PromptParser + PromptStateManager** です。  
`Do you want to allow ...` や `y/n` 系を approval、通常入力プロンプトを input として分離。  
根拠: [PromptParser.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Sources/cmux-core/PromptParser.swift), [PromptStateManager.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Sources/cmux-core/PromptStateManager.swift), [PromptParserTests.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Tests/cmux-coreTests/PromptParserTests.swift)
3. `OSC 9;4` は Running/Idle の補助には有効ですが、Waiting系の判定力は弱いです。  
根拠: [TerminalManager.swift](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Sources/cmux-core/TerminalManager.swift)

**2) Claude Code / Codex の実測 ESC**
1. Claude Code（ADR確認）  
`OSC 9;4`, `OSC 9`, `OSC 2/0` は emit、`OSC 133` は emit しない。  
根拠: [ADR](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/docs\/80_decisions\/ADR-20260301-osc-architecture.md#L21)
2. Codex CLI（`~/.codex` 実測ログ）  
TTY出力で `CSI ?2004h/?2004l`, `?1004h`, `?2026h/l`, `6n`、`OSC 10;?` を確認。  
根拠: [session log](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/.codex\/sessions\/2026\/02\/21\/rollout-2026-02-21T19-24-58-019c8360-ff8a-7273-bcc9-25ef53d66fd3.jsonl:417), [session log](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/.codex\/sessions\/2026\/02\/21\/rollout-2026-02-21T19-24-58-019c8360-ff8a-7273-bcc9-25ef53d66fd3.jsonl:425)
3. Codex で `OSC 133` の痕跡は未検出。  
`~/.codex/shell_snapshots`, `~/.codex/sessions`, `~/.codex/log` と codexバイナリ文字列を走査しても `133;A/B/C/D`, `]133;` なし。  
（推論）現行Codex CLIは `OSC 133` 依存設計に向かない。

**3) tmux pipe-pane で ESC 捕捉する実装方法**
1. 現状 `agtmux-tmux-v5` は `capture-pane` のみ。`pipe-pane` 未実装。  
根拠: [capture.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-tmux-v5\/src\/capture.rs:136)
2. 実装方針  
`tmux pipe-pane -o -O -t %pane 'agtmux-esc-tap --pane %pane --sock ...'`  
停止は `tmux pipe-pane -t %pane`（コマンド省略でclose）。
3. parser要件  
BEL/ST両終端、分割フレーム再組立、`DCS tmux passthrough (\ePtmux;...\e\)` unwrap、UTF-8壊れ耐性。
4. `pane_pipe` で占有検知し、既存ユーザー設定と衝突回避。  
tmuxは paneごと pipe 1本制約。  
根拠: tmux man `pipe-pane`, `pane_pipe`, `capture-pane`, `%output`, `allow-passthrough`

**4) WaitingInput 検出の最良手段**
1. **Claude**  
Tier1の hook/JSONL (`activity.waiting_input`) を正とする。
2. **Codex**  
Tier1だけでは `waiting_input` 明示が弱い。`thread.active/idle`, `turn.*` はあるが waiting_input直接なし。  
根拠: [projection.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-daemon-v5\/src\/projection.rs:673)
3. Codexは次の合成が最良。  
`turn.completed or thread.idle`（Tier1） + `prompt-ready signal`（Tier2: 画面/ESC/プロンプト） + `短時間無出力`。  
4. `WaitingApproval` は Codex AppServer `activeFlags: waitingOnApproval` を優先利用。  
根拠: [codex-appserver-api-reference.md](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/docs\/codex-appserver-api-reference.md:242), [codex_poller.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-runtime\/src\/codex_poller.rs:1587)

**5) OSC 133 で WaitingInput は取れるか**
結論: **不可（採用しない）**。  
理由: Claudeはemitしない（ADR明記）、Codexでも実測/ログ上の痕跡なし。さらに `OSC 133 A` は本質的に「シェルprompt開始」で、TUI内待機状態の一般解ではない。

**6) 提案FSM（3-tier統合）**
優先順位: `Error > WaitingApproval > WaitingInput > Running > Idle`

- `Error`  
Tier1: `activity.error`, `thread.error/systemError`, `turn.failed`
- `WaitingApproval`  
Tier1: `activity.waiting_approval`, Codex `activeFlags=waitingOnApproval`  
Tier2: approval prompt regex
- `WaitingInput`  
Tier1: `activity.waiting_input`  
Tier1+2合成: `(turn.completed|thread.idle) + prompt-ready`
- `Running`  
Tier1: `activity.running`, `thread.active`, `turn.started/inProgress`  
Tier2: `OSC 9;4 state=3`
- `Idle`  
Tier1: `activity.idle`, `thread.idle`, `turn.completed/interrupted`（prompt-readyなし）  
Tier2: `OSC 9;4 state=0`
- Tier3 fallback（低信頼）  
agent process生存 + read-blockヒント + 無出力時間で補助推定

**7) 実装優先順位と trade-off**
1. P0: `pipe-pane` tap基盤 + ESC parser + source health（最重要）
2. P1: Claude `OSC 9;4` の取り込み（Running/Idle edge高速化）
3. P2: Codex `activeFlags` 対応（WaitingApproval deterministic化）
4. P3: Codex `turn.completed + prompt-ready` 合成で WaitingInput 精度改善
5. P4: Tier3 process fallback（OS差分大、誤判定リスク高）

Trade-off:
- `pipe-pane` は pane占有衝突あり（`pane_pipe`管理必須）
- Prompt系は精度高いがプロバイダUI変更に脆い
- Process fallbackは移植性/信頼性が最も低い
- ESC不在を負の証拠にしない設計が必要

https://raw.githubusercontent.com/manaflow-ai/cmux/main/Sources/cmux-core/PromptStateManager.swift

**2) Claude Code / Codex の実測 ESC**
1. Claude Code（ADR確認）  
`OSC 9;4`, `OSC 9`, `OSC 2/0` は emit、`OSC 133` は emit しない。  
根拠: [ADR](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/docs\/80_decisions\/ADR-20260301-osc-architecture.md#L21)
2. Codex CLI（`~/.codex` 実測ログ）  
TTY出力で `CSI ?2004h/?2004l`, `?1004h`, `?2026h/l`, `6n`、`OSC 10;?` を確認。  
根拠: [session log](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/.codex\/sessions\/2026\/02\/21\/rollout-2026-02-21T19-24-58-019c8360-ff8a-7273-bcc9-25ef53d66fd3.jsonl:417), [session log](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/.codex\/sessions\/2026\/02\/21\/rollout-2026-02-21T19-24-58-019c8360-ff8a-7273-bcc9-25ef53d66fd3.jsonl:425)
3. Codex で `OSC 133` の痕跡は未検出。  
`~/.codex/shell_snapshots`, `~/.codex/sessions`, `~/.codex/log` と codexバイナリ文字列を走査しても `133;A/B/C/D`, `]133;` なし。  
（推論）現行Codex CLIは `OSC 133` 依存設計に向かない。

**3) tmux pipe-pane で ESC 捕捉する実装方法**
1. 現状 `agtmux-tmux-v5` は `capture-pane` のみ。`pipe-pane` 未実装。  
根拠: [capture.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-tmux-v5\/src\/capture.rs:136)
2. 実装方針  
`tmux pipe-pane -o -O -t %pane 'agtmux-esc-tap --pane %pane --sock ...'`  
停止は `tmux pipe-pane -t %pane`（コマンド省略でclose）。
3. parser要件  
BEL/ST両終端、分割フレーム再組立、`DCS tmux passthrough (\ePtmux;...\e\)` unwrap、UTF-8壊れ耐性。
4. `pane_pipe` で占有検知し、既存ユーザー設定と衝突回避。  
tmuxは paneごと pipe 1本制約。  
根拠: tmux man `pipe-pane`, `pane_pipe`, `capture-pane`, `%output`, `allow-passthrough`

**4) WaitingInput 検出の最良手段**
1. **Claude**  
Tier1の hook/JSONL (`activity.waiting_input`) を正とする。
2. **Codex**  
Tier1だけでは `waiting_input` 明示が弱い。`thread.active/idle`, `turn.*` はあるが waiting_input直接なし。  
根拠: [projection.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-daemon-v5\/src\/projection.rs:673)
3. Codexは次の合成が最良。  
`turn.completed or thread.idle`（Tier1） + `prompt-ready signal`（Tier2: 画面/ESC/プロンプト） + `短時間無出力`。  
4. `WaitingApproval` は Codex AppServer `activeFlags: waitingOnApproval` を優先利用。  
根拠: [codex-appserver-api-reference.md](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/docs\/codex-appserver-api-reference.md:242), [codex_poller.rs](\/Users\/virtualmachine\/ghq\/github.com\/g960059\/agtmux\/crates\/agtmux-runtime\/src\/codex_poller.rs:1587)

**5) OSC 133 で WaitingInput は取れるか**
結論: **不可（採用しない）**。  
理由: Claudeはemitしない（ADR明記）、Codexでも実測/ログ上の痕跡なし。さらに `OSC 133 A` は本質的に「シェルprompt開始」で、TUI内待機状態の一般解ではない。

**6) 提案FSM（3-tier統合）**
優先順位: `Error > WaitingApproval > WaitingInput > Running > Idle`

- `Error`  
Tier1: `activity.error`, `thread.error/systemError`, `turn.failed`
- `WaitingApproval`  
Tier1: `activity.waiting_approval`, Codex `activeFlags=waitingOnApproval`  
Tier2: approval prompt regex
- `WaitingInput`  
Tier1: `activity.waiting_input`  
Tier1+2合成: `(turn.completed|thread.idle) + prompt-ready`
- `Running`  
Tier1: `activity.running`, `thread.active`, `turn.started/inProgress`  
Tier2: `OSC 9;4 state=3`
- `Idle`  
Tier1: `activity.idle`, `thread.idle`, `turn.completed/interrupted`（prompt-readyなし）  
Tier2: `OSC 9;4 state=0`
- Tier3 fallback（低信頼）  
agent process生存 + read-blockヒント + 無出力時間で補助推定

**7) 実装優先順位と trade-off**
1. P0: `pipe-pane` tap基盤 + ESC parser + source health（最重要）
2. P1: Claude `OSC 9;4` の取り込み（Running/Idle edge高速化）
3. P2: Codex `activeFlags` 対応（WaitingApproval deterministic化）
4. P3: Codex `turn.completed + prompt-ready` 合成で WaitingInput 精度改善
5. P4: Tier3 process fallback（OS差分大、誤判定リスク高）

Trade-off:
- `pipe-pane` は pane占有衝突あり（`pane_pipe`管理必須）
- Prompt系は精度高いがプロバイダUI変更に脆い
- Process fallbackは移植性/信頼性が最も低い
- ESC不在を負の証拠にしない設計が必要
